sakuya_b6_clear_all_marked_power_bloc_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target }
		remove_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = sakuya_mark_power_bloc_target
		}
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_apply_modifier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		power_bloc = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_power_bloc = yes
			root = {
				has_variable = $VAR$
			}
		}
		power_bloc = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = $MOD$
				multiplier = root.var:$VAR$
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_change_marked_power_bloc_modifier_delta_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = $VAR$ } }
				set_variable = { name = $VAR$ value = 0 }
			}
			change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = $DELTA$ }
			if = {
				limit = { var:$VAR$ = 0 }
				remove_variable = $VAR$
			}
			sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_apply_modifier_effect = {
				MOD = $MOD$
				VAR = $VAR$
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_clear_marked_power_bloc_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			remove_variable = $VAR$
			power_bloc = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_set_marked_power_bloc_bool_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = { add_modifier = { name = $MOD$ } }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_clear_marked_power_bloc_bool_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_apply_marked_power_bloc_timed_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = {
				remove_modifier = $MOD$
				add_modifier = {
					name = $MOD$
					months = 1
					multiplier = $VALUE$
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_clear_marked_power_bloc_timed_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = { is_in_power_bloc = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = { remove_modifier = $MOD$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_set_marked_power_bloc_cohesion_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = {
				add_cohesion_number = -999999
				add_cohesion_number = $VALUE$
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_leverage_to_player_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = {
				add_leverage = { target = root value = $VALUE$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_leverage_to_marked_country_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope = {
			power_bloc = {
				add_leverage = { target = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope value = $VALUE$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_join_marked_power_bloc_player_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		join_power_bloc = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_join_marked_power_bloc_marked_country_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			join_power_bloc = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_invite_player_to_marked_power_bloc_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope }
		join_power_bloc = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_invite_marked_country_to_marked_power_bloc_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_leader_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			join_power_bloc = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_leader_scope
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_force_regime_change_marked_country_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		regime_change = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = installed_regime
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.2
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = -25
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = root
				}
				add_liberty_desire = 10
			}
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope = {
			add_cohesion_percent = scaled_cohesion_cost
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_force_state_religion_marked_country_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			set_state_religion = root.religion
			capital = {
				convert_population = {
					target = root.religion
					value = 0.05
				}
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { is_capital = no }
				convert_population = {
					target = root.religion
					value = 0.02
				}
			}
			state_religion_switch_effect = yes
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope = {
			add_cohesion_percent = scaled_cohesion_cost
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_power_bloc_culture_marked_country_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_power_bloc_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		ordered_primary_culture = {
			order_by = "root.culture_percent_country(this)"
			save_scope_as = main_culture_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			add_primary_culture = scope:main_culture_scope
			capital = {
				every_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = {
							OR = {
								shares_language_trait_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
								shares_language_trait_group_with_any_primary_culture = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					change_pop_culture = {
						target = scope:main_culture_scope
						value = 0.05
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_power_bloc_scope = {
			add_cohesion_percent = scaled_cohesion_cost
		}
	}
}

# ============================================================
# B6 TAB1 - STATE
# ============================================================

sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_owner_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target } }
		random_state = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = {
			owner = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_owner_scope }
			state_region = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_change_selected_state_region_involvement_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		add_involvement = {
			strategic_region = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope.region
			value = $VALUE$
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_claim_selected_state_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_owner_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope
	if = {
		limit = { any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target } }
		random_state = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = {
			owner = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_owner_scope }
			state_region = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_player_state_sources_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_player_primary_culture_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_player_religion_scope
	random_primary_culture = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_player_primary_culture_scope }
	religion = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_player_religion_scope }
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_player_capital_state_region_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_player_capital_state_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_player_capital_state_region_scope
	capital = {
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_player_capital_state_scope
		state_region = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_player_capital_state_region_scope }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_state_sources_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_marked_country_primary_culture_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_marked_country_religion_scope
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			random_primary_culture = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_country_primary_culture_scope }
			religion = { save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_country_religion_scope }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_selected_state_fixed_modifier_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = {
			remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
			add_modifier = { name = $MOD$ days = -1 }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_clear_selected_state_fixed_modifier_group_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = {
			remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
			remove_modifier = $MOD1$
			remove_modifier = $MOD2$
			remove_modifier = $MOD3$
			remove_modifier = $MOD4$
		}
	}
}

	every_scope_state = {
		remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
		add_modifier = { name = $MOD$ days = -1 }
	}
}

		remove_modifier = $MOD1$
		remove_modifier = $MOD2$
		remove_modifier = $MOD3$
		remove_modifier = $MOD4$
	}
}

		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_fixed_modifier_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes

			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
				owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope

		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
				add_modifier = { name = $MOD$ days = -1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

}

sakuya_b6_apply_selected_building_group_employee_state_default_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_oil_rig } sakuya_b6_add_fixed_modifier_to_all_marked_states_effect = { OLD_MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_oil_extraction MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_oil_extraction_default } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_coal_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_iron_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_lead_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_sulfur_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_gold_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_bauxite_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_copper_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_commonores_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_advancedores_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_natural_gas_rig has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_rare_earths_mine has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_uranium_mine } } sakuya_b6_add_fixed_modifier_to_all_marked_states_effect = { OLD_MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_mining MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_mining_default } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_rubber_plantation } sakuya_b6_add_fixed_modifier_to_all_marked_states_effect = { OLD_MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_rubber MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_rubber_default } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_paper_mill has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_glassworks has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_tooling_workshop } } sakuya_b6_add_fixed_modifier_to_all_marked_states_effect = { OLD_MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_light_industry MOD = sakuya_cheat_b6_reserved_build_employee_bg_light_industry_default } }

sakuya_b6_add_stacking_modifier_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
			remove_modifier = $OLD1$
			remove_modifier = $OLD2$
			remove_modifier = $OLD3$
			remove_modifier = $OLD4$

			if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C1A$ has_modifier = $C1B$ has_modifier = $C1C$ has_modifier = $C1D$ has_modifier = $C1E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD1$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C2A$ has_modifier = $C2B$ has_modifier = $C2C$ has_modifier = $C2D$ has_modifier = $C2E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD2$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C3A$ has_modifier = $C3B$ has_modifier = $C3C$ has_modifier = $C3D$ has_modifier = $C3E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD3$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C4A$ has_modifier = $C4B$ has_modifier = $C4C$ has_modifier = $C4D$ has_modifier = $C4E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD4$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C5A$ has_modifier = $C5B$ has_modifier = $C5C$ has_modifier = $C5D$ has_modifier = $C5E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD5$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C6A$ has_modifier = $C6B$ has_modifier = $C6C$ has_modifier = $C6D$ has_modifier = $C6E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD6$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C7A$ has_modifier = $C7B$ has_modifier = $C7C$ has_modifier = $C7D$ has_modifier = $C7E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD7$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C8A$ has_modifier = $C8B$ has_modifier = $C8C$ has_modifier = $C8D$ has_modifier = $C8E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD8$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C9A$ has_modifier = $C9B$ has_modifier = $C9C$ has_modifier = $C9D$ has_modifier = $C9E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD9$ days = -1 } }

			else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C10A$ has_modifier = $C10B$ has_modifier = $C10C$ has_modifier = $C10D$ has_modifier = $C10E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD10$ days = -1 } }

		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_stacking_modifier_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope } }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				remove_modifier = $OLD_MOD$
				remove_modifier = $OLD1$
				remove_modifier = $OLD2$
				remove_modifier = $OLD3$
				remove_modifier = $OLD4$

				if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C1A$ has_modifier = $C1B$ has_modifier = $C1C$ has_modifier = $C1D$ has_modifier = $C1E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD1$ days = -1 } }

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C2A$ has_modifier = $C2B$ has_modifier = $C2C$ has_modifier = $C2D$ has_modifier = $C2E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD2$ days = -1 } }

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C3A$ has_modifier = $C3B$ has_modifier = $C3C$ has_modifier = $C3D$ has_modifier = $C3E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD3$ days = -1 } }

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C4A$ has_modifier = $C4B$ has_modifier = $C4C$ has_modifier = $C4D$ has_modifier = $C4E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD4$ days = -1 } }

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C5A$ has_modifier = $C5B$ has_modifier = $C5C$ has_modifier = $C5D$ has_modifier = $C5E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD5$ days = -1 } }

				else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_modifier = $C6A$ has_modifier = $C6B$ has_modifier = $C6C$ has_modifier = $C6D$ has_modifier = $C6E$ } } } add_modifier = { name = $ADD6$ days = -1 } }

sakuya_b6_add_selected_state_fixed_modifier_months_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = {
			remove_modifier = $MOD$
			add_modifier = { name = $MOD$ months = $MONTHS$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_set_selected_state_type_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_scope = { set_state_type = $TYPE$ }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_set_all_player_states_type_effect = {
	every_scope_state = {
		set_state_type = $TYPE$
	}
}

sakuya_b6_change_selected_state_arable_land_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { add_arable_land = $VALUE$ }
	}
}

	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { add_devastation = $VALUE$ }
	}
}

	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { add_pollution = $VALUE$ }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_change_all_player_state_pollution_effect = {
	every_scope_state = {
		state_region = { add_pollution = $VALUE$ }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_force_selected_state_resource_discovery_effect = {
}

sakuya_b6_force_selected_state_resource_depletion_effect = {
}

sakuya_b6_add_state_trait_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			state_region = { add_state_trait = $TRAIT$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_state_trait_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			any_state = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
				owner = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				state_region = { add_state_trait = $TRAIT$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_remove_state_trait_from_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			state_region = { remove_state_trait = $TRAIT$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_remove_state_trait_from_marked_country_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_country = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			any_state = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
				owner = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				state_region = { remove_state_trait = $TRAIT$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_remove_state_trait_from_all_valid_targets_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_remove_state_trait_from_player_marked_states_effect = { TRAIT = $TRAIT$ }
	sakuya_b6_remove_state_trait_from_marked_country_states_effect = { TRAIT = $TRAIT$ }
}

sakuya_b6_add_player_homeland_to_selected_state_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		every_primary_culture = {
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { is_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope } } }
				scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { add_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_remove_player_homeland_from_selected_state_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope }
		every_primary_culture = {
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope
			if = {
				limit = { scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { is_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope } }
				scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { remove_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_add_marked_country_homeland_to_selected_state_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_selected_state_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_state_sources_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_primary_culture = {
				save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { is_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope } } }
					scope:sakuya_b6_selected_state_region_scope = { add_homeland = scope:sakuya_b6_temp_primary_culture_scope }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

}

sakuya_b6_move_player_unemployed_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_pop = {
			limit = { is_employed = no }
			root = {
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
					save_temporary_scope_as = sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state }
				move_partial_pop = {
					state = scope:sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state
					population_ratio = $VALUE$
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_move_marked_country_unemployed_to_marked_foreign_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
				owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			}
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = { is_employed = no }
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
						owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
					}
					save_temporary_scope_as = sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state
				}
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state }
					move_partial_pop = {
						state = scope:sakuya_b6_unemployed_migration_target_state
						population_ratio = $VALUE$
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popmig_resolve_marked_country_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_to_player_integrated_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_popmig_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope
			any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_pop = {
				root = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
						save_temporary_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state }
					move_partial_pop = { state = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state population_ratio = $VALUE$ }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popmig_player_to_marked_integrated_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_popmig_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope
			scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope = { any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		}
		every_scope_pop = {
			scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_marked_country_scope = {
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
					save_temporary_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state }
				move_partial_pop = { state = scope:sakuya_b6_popmig_target_state population_ratio = $VALUE$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = aristocrats qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = bureaucrats qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = capitalists qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = clergymen qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = clerks qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = engineers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = farmers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = laborers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = machinists qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = officers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = peasants qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = shopkeepers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = slaves qualifications = $VALUE$ }
				set_pop_qualifications = { pop_type = soldiers qualifications = $VALUE$ }
			}
		}
	}
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }

	every_scope_state = {
		add_loyalists_in_state = { value = -1.0 }
		add_radicals_in_state = { value = -1.0 }
	}
}

	sakuya_b6_resolve_player_state_sources_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_player_religion_scope }
		every_scope_state = {
			convert_population = { target = scope:sakuya_b6_player_religion_scope value = $VALUE$ }
		}
	}

# === B6 TAB2 POP MOD STACK EFFECTS END ===

sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_choice_effect = {
	remove_variable = $INDEX_VAR$
	remove_variable = $VALUE_VAR$
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_set_choice_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = $INDEX_VAR$ value = $INDEX$ }
	set_variable = { name = $VALUE_VAR$ value = $VALUE$ }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_set_culture_mode_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode value = $MODE$ }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_set_culture_player_effect = {
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_player_selected value = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode value = 1 }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_set_culture_marked_effect = {
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_player_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected value = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode value = 2 }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_set_culture_selected_effect = {
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_player_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected value = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode value = 3 }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_selected_culture_effect = {
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode var:sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode = 3 }
		remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_selected_culture_all_effect = {
	sakuya_list_culture_clear_selection_vars_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_selected_culture_effect = yes
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_culture_choice_effect = {
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_player_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected
	remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_mode
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_start_drag_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = sakuya_b6_popgen_slider_dragging value = 1 }
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_stop_drag_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_slider_dragging }
		remove_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_slider_dragging
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_popgen_player_country_scope
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_player_country_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_popgen_player_country_scope
	save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popgen_player_country_scope
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_marked_country_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_country_target }
		save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_target_culture_effect = {
	clear_saved_scope = sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_player_selected }
		ordered_primary_culture = {
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected }
		sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
			scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
				ordered_primary_culture = {
					position = 0
					check_range_bounds = no
					save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_selected_selected has_variable = sakuya_list_confirmed_culture }
		var:sakuya_list_confirmed_culture = {
			save_scope_as = sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_apply_literacy_and_culture_effect = {
	every_scope_pop = {
		limit = { culture = cu:sakuya_nonexistent }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 1 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.1 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 2 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.2 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 3 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.3 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 4 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.4 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 5 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.5 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 6 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.6 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 7 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.7 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 8 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.8 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 9 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 0.9 } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index = 10 } set_pop_literacy = { literacy_rate = 1.0 } }
		change_pop_culture = { value = 1 target = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
			NOT = { has_cultural_community = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope }
		}
		add_cultural_community = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
	}
}

		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_player_country_scope scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_player_country_scope = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_culture_marked_selected } exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		if = { limit = { scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = { country_has_state_religion = rel:catholic } } create_pop = { religion = catholic culture = sakuya_nonexistent size = $SIZE$ working_adult_ratio = $RATIO$ } }

		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope root = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_amount_index has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_workforce_index } }

	}
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_player_country_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_target_culture_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_amount_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_workforce_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index
			any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
		}
		random_scope_state = { limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_in_current_state_effect = yes }
	}
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_to_player_homeland_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_player_country_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_target_culture_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_amount_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_workforce_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index
			any_scope_state = { is_homeland_of_country_cultures = root }
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { is_homeland_of_country_cultures = root }
			sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_in_current_state_effect = yes
		}
	}
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
}

sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_player_country_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	sakuya_b6_popgen_resolve_target_culture_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_popgen_target_culture_scope
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_amount_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_workforce_index
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_popgen_literacy_index
			any_state = {
				has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target
				owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			}
		}
		random_state = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope } sakuya_b6_popgen_spawn_in_current_state_effect = yes }
	}
sakuya_b6_popgen_clear_runtime_effect = yes
}

sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 0 has_building = $BUILDING$ } remove_building = $BUILDING$ }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 1 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 2 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 3 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 5 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 4 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 5 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 15 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 6 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 20 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 7 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 30 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 8 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 50 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 9 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 75 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 10 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 100 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 11 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 150 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 12 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 200 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 13 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 300 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 14 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 500 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 15 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 1000 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 16 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 10000 } }
	if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 17 } create_building = { building = $BUILDING$ level = 1000000 } }
}

sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_target_integrated any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
			sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				sakuya_b6_build_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_build_potential_capped_apply_to_current_state_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 0 has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ }
		state_region = { remove_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 1 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 1 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 2 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 2 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 3 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 5 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 4 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 10 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 5 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 15 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 6 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 20 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 7 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 30 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 8 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 50 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 9 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 75 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 10 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 100 } } }

	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 12 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 200 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 13 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 300 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 14 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 500 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 15 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 1000 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 16 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 10000 } } }
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 17 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 1000000 } } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 0 } NOT = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } }
		state_region = { add_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 2 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 1 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 3 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 4 } } }

		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 7 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 29 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 8 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 49 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 9 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 74 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 10 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 99 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 11 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 149 } } }

		state_region = { add_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ }
	}
}

# === CMP + TECH & RES V0.2 RESOURCE OPERATIONS START ===
# Numeric SET: replace current resource potential with the exact slider value.
sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } state_region = { remove_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 0 } }
		state_region = { add_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 2 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 1 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 3 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 4 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 4 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 9 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 5 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 14 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 6 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 19 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 7 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 29 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 8 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 49 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 9 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 74 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 10 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 99 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 11 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 149 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 12 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 199 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 13 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 299 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 14 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 499 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 15 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 999 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 16 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 9999 } } }
		if = { limit = { root.var:sakuya_b6_build_level_index = 17 } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = 999999 } } }
	}
}
sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } state_region = { remove_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ } }
}
sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_target_integrated any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
			sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
}
sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_level_index
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				sakuya_b6_build_potential_set_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
			}
		}
	}
}
sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_player_marked_states_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_target_integrated any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
			sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
		}
	}
}
sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_marked_country_states_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope
			any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope }
		}
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_rare_earths_mine } sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_marked_country_states_effect = { BUILDING = building_rare_earths_mine } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_uranium_mine } sakuya_b6_build_potential_clear_apply_to_marked_country_states_effect = { BUILDING = building_uranium_mine } }
}

sakuya_b6_techres_add_exact_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = $AMOUNT$ } } }
	else = {
		state_region = { add_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ }
		state_region = { change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = $DELTA$ } }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_techres_set_exact_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } state_region = { remove_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ } }
	state_region = {
		add_resource_potential = $BUILDING$
		change_resource_potential = { type = $BUILDING$ amount = $DELTA$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT1$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT1$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT2$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT2$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT3$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT3$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT4$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT4$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT5$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT5$ }
		add_state_trait = $TRAIT$
	}
}

sakuya_b6_techres_clear_exact_current_state_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_potential_resource = $BUILDING$ } state_region = { remove_resource_potential = $BUILDING$ } }
	state_region = {
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT1$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT1$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT2$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT2$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT3$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT3$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT4$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT4$ }
		if = { limit = { has_state_trait = $TRAIT5$ } remove_state_trait = $TRAIT5$ }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_techres_add_apply_to_player_targets_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_target_integrated any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { is_incorporated = yes }
			sakuya_b6_techres_add_exact_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ AMOUNT = $AMOUNT$ DELTA = $DELTA$ }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state }
			sakuya_b6_techres_add_exact_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ AMOUNT = $AMOUNT$ DELTA = $DELTA$ }
		}
	}
}

sakuya_b6_techres_add_apply_to_marked_country_targets_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope } }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target }
				sakuya_b6_techres_add_exact_current_state_effect = { BUILDING = $BUILDING$ AMOUNT = $AMOUNT$ DELTA = $DELTA$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_sel_uranium_mine } sakuya_b6_techres_clear_apply_to_marked_country_targets_effect = { BUILDING = building_uranium_mine TRAIT1 = state_trait_small_uranium_mine TRAIT2 = state_trait_medium_uranium_mine TRAIT3 = state_trait_big_uranium_mine TRAIT4 = state_trait_big_uranium_mine TRAIT5 = state_trait_big_uranium_mine } }
}

sakuya_b6_techres_preset_mining_player_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = sakuya_b6_build_target_integrated any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes } }
		every_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } sakuya_b6_techres_preset_mining_current_state_effect = yes }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_scope_state = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } }
		every_scope_state = { limit = { has_decree = decree_sakuya_mark_state } sakuya_b6_techres_preset_mining_current_state_effect = yes }
	}
}

sakuya_b6_techres_preset_mining_marked_effect = {
	sakuya_b6_resolve_marked_country_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope any_state = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target owner = scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope } }
		scope:sakuya_b6_marked_country_scope = { every_scope_state = { limit = { has_variable = sakuya_mark_foreign_state_target } sakuya_b6_techres_preset_mining_current_state_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# === CMP + TECH & RES V0.2 RESOURCE OPERATIONS END ===
# === B6 BUILD PLACEMENT END ===

sakuya_b6_country_self_apply_modifier_effect = {
	remove_modifier = $MOD$
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = $VAR$ }
		add_modifier = {
			name = $MOD$
			multiplier = var:$VAR$
		}
		set_variable = $ACTIVE$
	}
	if = {
		limit = { var:$VAR$ = 0 }
		remove_modifier = $MOD$
		remove_variable = $VAR$
		remove_variable = $ACTIVE$
	}
}

sakuya_b6_country_self_modifier_delta_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = $VAR$ } }
		set_variable = { name = $VAR$ value = 0 }
	}
	change_variable = { name = $VAR$ add = $DELTA$ }
	sakuya_b6_country_self_apply_modifier_effect = {
		MOD = $MOD$
		VAR = $VAR$
		ACTIVE = $ACTIVE$
	}
}

sakuya_b6_country_self_modifier_clear_effect = {
	remove_modifier = $MOD$
	remove_variable = $VAR$
	remove_variable = $ACTIVE$
}
